CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介


                    China Modern Dairy Holdings Limited (the “Company” or
                    “Modern Dairy” together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is
                    the leading dairy farming operator and fresh raw milk producer
                    in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). Modern Dairy
                    operates 26 dairy farms in the PRC with dairy cows over 247
                    thousand heads and annual milk yield approximately 1.49 million
                    tons. The Company initiated the production model of “Zero-
                    distance Integration of Forage Grass Planting, Cows Breeding
                    and Milk Processing within Two Hours”, which is the first and
                    the only enterprise at present in the PRC that applies integrated
                    production model with SGS certification. Modern Dairy’s
                    products have been awarded the Gold Prize of Monde Selection
                    which is generally regarded as the “Nobel Prize” in the food
                    industry, for the past seven consecutive years.



                    中國現代牧業控股有限公司（  「本公司」或「現代牧業」連同其附屬公
                    司（
                     「本集團」  ），為中華人民共和國（「中國」）乳牛畜牧營運商及原
                    料奶生產商的翹楚。現代牧業於中國經營26個乳牛牧場，擁有超
                    過24.7萬頭乳牛及每年產奶量約1.49百萬噸。本公司首創「種養加
                    一體化、零距離2小時」的生產模式，是中國首家、也是目前唯一
                    一家一體化生產模式並通過SGS認證的企業。現代牧業的產品於
                    過去七年連續在素有食品業「諾貝爾獎」之稱的世界食品品質評鑒
                    大會中獲得金獎。
“Farming diligently only for purity,
		 genuineness, freshness and vitality”
                 「默默耕耘，只為純、真、鮮、活」

 difficult times in the industry, but also demonstrated its advantages   可持續健康發展。本人僅代表中國現代牧業控股
 during the upward cycle of the industry, significantly improving its    有限公司董事會，欣然向各位股東呈報現代牧業

 of sustainable development. On behalf of the board of directors         績。
 of China Modern Dairy Holdings Ltd., I am pleased to present the
 annual results of Modern Dairy for the year ended 31 December

 As the leading dairy farming enterprise in the PRC, Modern Dairy,       作為國內奶牛養殖企業的翹楚，現代牧業在成

 and Coca-Cola. We focused on management innovation, centered                            於打造數字化牧場，做牧業精細化管理的「鮮」行

further reduce the content of furosine in milk, subject to eligibility

Heat Stress Alleviation Measures                                         緩解熱應激措施
Heat stress as a result of high temperature in summer has become         由於夏季高溫所帶來的熱應激對乳牛的影響已成
a seasonal problem for milkable cows. The yield of each milkable         為一個季節性問題，每頭成乳牛的產量一般會
cow is generally slightly reduced due to the impact caused by            受熱應激影響略有下降。為緩解熱應激帶來的影
heat stress. To minimize the impact, the Group has implemented           響，本集團已採取緩解計劃。本集團引進高效通
alleviation plans. The Group equipped cowshed with efficient             風系統設備裝備牛舍，如強力的風扇，升級牛舍
ventilation system, such as powerful fans, upgraded the facilities in    設施，加強每頭奶牛的健康水平。
barns and enhanced the health of each dairy cow.

 FINANCIAL MANAGEMENT POLICIES                                         財務管理政策
 The Group continued to closely manage financial risks to safeguard    本集團持續嚴密控制財務風險以保障本公司股東
 the interest of the shareholders of the Company. The Group            權益。本集團利用經營所得現金流量、銀行貸
 applied its cash flows generated from operations, bank loans and      款，發行債券及本公司普通股股份所得款項以應
 proceeds from the issuance of bonds and ordinary shares of the        付其經營及投資需要。

committed to upholding a high standard of corporate governance        維護本公司股東（ 「股東」

to safeguard the interests of the shareholders of the Company (the    目標。

(“CG Code”) as set out in Appendix 14 to the Rules Governing the      企業管治報告（  「企業管治守則」 ）中的守則條文。

(the “Listing Rules”). The Company has, throughout the year           及直至本年報日期，本公司已遵守載於企業管治
ended 31 December 2020 (the “Reporting Year”) and up to the           守則的守則條文，惟守則條文第A.6.7條的偏離除
date of this annual report, complied with the code provisions set     外。
out in the CG Code except for the deviation from code provisions

THE BOARD                                                             董事會
Role of Directors                                                     董事之角色
The Company is headed by the Board which assumes the                  本公司以負責領導及監控的董事會為首，並共同
responsibility for leadership and control and be collectively         負責指導並監督本公司事務以促使本公司成功
responsible for promoting the success of the Company by directing     發展。董事會向股東負責，以負責任及有效之方
and supervising the Company’s affairs. The Board is accountable       式管理本公司。每名董事均致力為本公司之最佳
to the Shareholders for managing the Company in a responsible         利益行事並以其專業技能及知識為本公司作出貢
and effective manner. Every Director is committed to act in the       獻。董事會決定及實施整體策略，並代表股東監
best interest of the Company and to contribute their expertise        察本集團之表現。
and knowledge to the Company. The Board decides on overall
strategies and monitors the Group’s performance on behalf of the
Shareholders.

THE BOARD’S DIVERSITY POLICY                                            董事會多元化政策
The Board has adopted diversity policy, which sets out the              董事會已採納多元化政策，其中載有董事會成員
approach to diversity of Board members. In determining the              多元化之方針。本公司在釐定董事會成員組合
Board’s composition, the Company considers a range of diversity         時，會從多方面考慮董事會成員多元化，包括但
elements, including but not limited to gender, age, cultural            不限於性別、年齡、文化及教育背景、專業經
and education background, professional experience, skills and           驗、技能及知識。董事會所有委任均以用人唯才
knowledge. All appointments of the Board will be made based             為原則，並以客觀準則考慮人選，充分顧及董事
on merit and objective criteria while taking into full account of the   會多元化的裨益。
interest of Board’s diversity.

with both gender, three of which are independent non-executive          中三名為獨立非執行董事，藉此促進管理過程的

regulations. The components of the framework are shown as follow:

     objectives, forming a basis for determining how risks should       何管理風險提供基礎。

Handling and Dissemination of Inside Information                      處理及發佈內幕消息
The Group has formulated an internal code on Information              本集團已制訂關於信息披露的內部守則確保本集
Disclosure to ensure the truthfulness, accuracy, completeness and     團信息披露真實、準確、完整、及時並符合證券
timeliness of information disclosure of the Group in accordance       及期貨條例及上市規則要求。內部守則在以下方
with the requirements of the SFO and the Listing Rules. The           面進行了詳細規定。
Internal Code provides for detailed rules in the following areas.

–    basic principles of information disclosure                       –   信息披露的基本原則

–    information that needs to be disclosed and the criteria          –   需要披露的信息及其標準
     thereof

–    duties of the parties responsible for information disclosure     –   負責信息披露人員的職責

–    procedures for the circulation, review and disclosure of         –   傳閱、審閱及披露信息的程序
     information

–    confidentiality of the undisclosed information                   –   未披露信息的保密

–    information communication system with investors, securities      –   與投資者、證券分析師及媒體等各方的信息
     analysts and the media, etc.                                         交流制度

–    accountability and handling measures                             –   問責及處理措施

 COMPLIANCE WITH LAWS AND                                                遵守法律及法規
 REGULATIONS
 The Group’s main operations are carried out by the Company’s            本集團的主要營運由本公司中國內地附屬公司進
 subsidiaries in the mainland China while the Company itself is          行，而本公司自身於香港聯交所上市。本集團營
 listed on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange. The Group’s operations          運受香港及中國內地法例監管。於本匯報年度及

Employee Partnership Program                                          員工合夥人計畫
As the Company’s business scale continues to expand, operating        隨著本公司業務規模的不斷擴展，經營管理及激
management and incentive system shall keep up with the                勵制度需要與時俱進，傳統的「制度管理+目標實
times. The traditional rationale of management by objectives          現」的管理有其局限性。因此，如何激發員工積極
has limitations. How to ignite employees’ working enthusiasm          服務公司、增強員工主人翁意識、與公司共同發
to serve the company proactively, and enhance their sense of          展，是公司需要深入考慮的。為此，本公司在現
ownership and strive for the development of the company together      有限股獎勵計畫外，特別制定了員工合夥人盈利
are the topics that the Company needs to cope with in depth.          分享激勵計劃（「員工合夥人計劃」
                                                                                     ），以打造員工
Therefore, on top of the existing restricted share award schemes,     與公司的利益共同體。
the Company has specially formulated an employee partnership
profit-sharing program (the “Employee Partnership Program”)
with aim to build a community of shared interests amongst the
Company and its employees.

Many leading enterprises in mainland China realized that the          眾多國內龍頭企業也意識到合夥人制度與公司的
partnership systems are closely correlated to a sustainable           長遠發展是息息相關的，因此，紛紛推行合夥人
development of the company, and start to implement the                管理模式，讓員工與公司形成利益共同體、事業
partnership management system one after another, allowing             共同體、命運的共同體。
employees and company to build a community of shared interests,
business and future.

The Employee Partnership Program is a model of contemporary           員工合夥人計劃是一種融合激勵與管理的模式，
management that combines incentives and management.                   借助公司平台，以利益激勵合夥人，賦予其更多
Leveraging the platform provided by the Company, the program          權利，並相應承擔更多為公司做貢獻義務。
will motivate the partners on the basis of profit sharing while the
partners will be empowered with more rights as well as to bear
more responsibilities driving to the success of the Company.

The management concept and model of “Employee Partnership             本 公 司 的「員 工 合 夥 人 計 劃」管 理 理 念 與 管 理 模
Program“ includes two phases. The first phase is to implement the     式，首階段以「合夥人利潤分享」方式加以推行，
“employee profit sharing“ program, and the second phase is to         第二階段按「盈利分享」與「業績進步獎勵」兩類方
facilitate both “profit sharing“ and “performance progress reward”    案推行。隨著第一、二階的經驗積累，公司未來
program. As accumulating experience during the implementation         繼續深入推動更全面、科學的員工合夥人計劃。
of two phases, the Company will continue to launch a more
comprehensive and scientific Employee Partnership Program in the
future.

 PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES                                        主要風險及不確定因素
 The Group adopts a comprehensive risk management framework.              本集團採用全面風險管理框架。風險管理政策及
 Risk management policies and procedures are regularly reviewed           程序獲定期檢討並更新以應對乳品質量、食品安
 and updated to react changes in dairy product quality, food              全、生產、動物疾病、環境污染、市場開發及財
 safety, production, animal disease, environment pollution,               務風險變動。審核委員會檢討風險相關政策及仔
 market developments and financial risks. The Audit Committee             細檢查管理層是否已履行其職責，設立對監控及
 reviews risk-related policies and scrutinizes that management has        控制本集團業務活動、外部風險及監管環境變動
 performed its duty to have effective risk management and internal        產生的風險而言屬必要的風險管理及內部監控制
 control systems necessary for monitoring and controlling risks           度，並向董事會就上述情況作出報告。
 arising from the Group’s business activities, changing external risks
 and regulatory environment, and report to the Board on the above.

 Strategic Risks                                                          策略風險
 The Directors maintain a strategic plan based on the knowledge on        董事根據乳品行業知識維持策略計劃。本集團將
 the dairy industry. The Group will invest in good grass plantation,      投資優質草場、奶牛、乳製品及根據策略計劃推
 good dairy cows, quality milk production and promotion of                進綜合化經營以滿足市場需求及預期。本集團將
 integrated operation that based on the strategic plan in order to        密切檢討及監控短長期策略並致力為股東帶來最
 cope with the market demand and expectation. The Group will              佳利益。
 closely review and monitor its short and long-term strategies and
 strives for the best interests to its shareholders.

 Operational Risks                                                        營運風險
 Operational risk is concerned with possible losses caused by failure     營運風險與內部程序失效、人員、制度或外部事
 of internal processes, people, system or external events, such           項造成的可能損失，此風險透過建立健全的內部
 risk is mitigated by and controlled by establishing sound internal       監控、明確責任範圍、適當的職責分離及有效的
 control, setting out clear lines of responsibility, proper segregation   內部報告予以降低及控制。管理層定期檢討本集
 of duties and effective internal reporting. Management regularly         團營運以確保本集團虧損（不論是財務或其他方
 reviews the Group’s operations to ensure that the Group’s risk of        面）、欺詐所導致、錯誤、疏忽或其他營運及合規
 losses, whether financial or otherwise, resulting from fraud, errors,    事宜的風險得到充分管理。
 omissions and other operational and compliance matters, are
 adequately managed.

 Financial Risks                                                          財務風險
 The Group manages its foreign exchange risk by performing                本集團通過定期審查外匯風險淨額，並可能訂立
 regular reviews of the Group’s net foreign exchange exposures            貨幣遠期合約或貨幣期權合約（如需要），以管理

 contracts, when necessary, to manage its foreign exchange                理層定期檢討及評估本集團的利率變動風險，並
 exposure. Furthermore, with aim to tackle the risk of interest rate      可考慮在必要時訂立利率掉期合約或其他對沖策
 fluctuation, the management regularly reviews and assesses the           略。
 Group’s exposure to interest rate movement and may consider
 entering into interest rate swap contracts or other hedging
 strategies when necessary.

OPINION                                                                意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of China         我們已審核載於第104至254頁中國現代牧業控股

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true      我們認為，綜合財務報表已根據國際會計準則理
and fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as   事會（「國際會計準則理事會」  ）頒佈的國際財務報

BASIS FOR OPINION                                                      意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards          我們根據香港會計師公會（「香港會計師公會」

 4. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                         4. 重要會計政策
      Basis of consolidation                                                  綜合基準
      The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                   綜合財務報表載有本公司以及由本公司及其
      financial statements of the Company and entities (including             附屬公司控制的實體（包括結構性實體）的財
      structured entities) controlled by the Company and its                  務報表。倘屬以下情況，則本公司獲得控制
      subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the Company:                     權：

      (i)     has power over the investee;                                    (i)     可對被投資者行使權力；

      (ii)    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its         (ii)    因參與被投資者的業務而可獲得或有
              involvement with the investee; and                                      權獲得可變回報；及

      (iii)   has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.         (iii)   有能力藉行使其權力而影響該等回
                                                                                      報。

      The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee             倘有事實或情況顯示上述三項控制因素中，
      if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to           有一項或以上出現變數，本集團會重新評估
      one or more of the three elements of control listed above.              其是否控制被投資者。

   like transactions and events in similar circumstances. Under        成本於綜合財務狀況表確認，並於其後就確

      Leases                                                              租賃
      Definition of a lease                                               租賃的定義
      A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the     倘合約授予權利以代價為交換在某一時期內
      right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of     控制使用已識別資產，則該合約屬於租賃或
      time in exchange for consideration.                                 包含租賃。

      For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date         就於初次應用日期或之後訂立或修訂或業務
      of initial application, or arising from business combinations,      合併產生的合約而言，本集團根據國際財務
      the Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a              報告準則第16號於開始修訂日期或收購日期
      lease based on the definition under IFRS 16 at inception,           的定義評估合約是否屬於或包含租賃。除非
      modification date or acquisition date. Such contract will not       合約條款及條件其後改變，否則不得重新評
      be reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the contract       估該合約。
      are subsequently changed.

   option. It also applies the recognition exemption for lease of                       價值資產租賃的租賃付款於租期內以直線法
   low-value assets. Lease payments on short-term leases and                            或另一系統基準確認為開支。
   leases of low-value assets are recognised as expense on a
   straight-line basis or another systematic basis over the lease
   term.

   Right-of-use assets                                                                  使用權資產
   The cost of right-of-use assets includes:                                            使用權資產的成本包括：

   •      the amount of the initial measurement of the lease                            •   租賃負債的初步計量金額；
          liability;

   •      any lease payments made at or before the                                      •   於開始日期或之前所作的任何租賃付
          commencement date, less any lease incentives received;                            款，減任何已收取的租賃優惠；

   •      any initial direct costs incurred by the Group; and                           •   本集團產生的任何初步直接成本；及

   •      an estimate of costs to be incurred by the Group                              •   本集團在拆除及移除相關資產、恢復
          in dismantling and removing the underlying assets,                                其所在地點或將相關資產恢復至租賃
          restoring the site on which it is located or restoring the                        條款及條件所要求的狀況時所產生的
          underlying asset to the condition required by the terms                           估計成本。
          and conditions of the lease.

   R i g h t-o f-u s e a s s e t s a r e m e a s u r e d a t c o s t, l e s s a n y     使用權資產按成本減任何累計折舊及減值虧
   accumulated depreciation and impairment losses, and                                  損計量，並就租賃負債任何重新計量作出調
   adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.                                 整。

   Right-of-use assets are depreciated on a straight-line basis                         使用權資產按其估計可使用年期及租期中的
   over the shorter of its estimated useful life and the lease term.                    較短者以直線法折舊。

   The Group presents right-of-use assets as a separate line item                       本集團於綜合財務狀況表中將使用權資產獨
   on the consolidated statement of financial position.                                 立呈列。

      •      any payment made to the employee on the cancellation          •    任何向僱員作出的付款於有關授出註
             or settlement of the grant shall be accounted for as               銷或結算時將作為購回股權入賬（即從
             the repurchase of an equity interest, i.e. as a deduction          權益中扣除），惟倘該付款超過所授出
             from equity, except to the extent that the payment                 權益工具的公平值，則於購回日期計
             exceeds the fair value of the equity instruments granted,          量。任何有關超額將確認為開支。
             measured at the repurchase date. Any such excess shall
             be recognised as an expense.

      Taxation                                                             稅項
      Income tax expenses represent the sum of the tax currently           所得稅支出指即期應付稅項及遞延稅項總
      payable and deferred tax.                                            和。

      The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the         即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢利計算。由於
      year. Taxable profit differs from profit/loss before tax because     其他年度的應課稅或可扣稅收入或開支及毋
      of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other         須課稅或不可扣稅的項目，故應課稅溢利與
      years and items that are never taxable or deductible. The            除稅前溢利╱虧損不同。本集團的即期稅項
      Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates      負債按報告期末已頒佈或實際上已頒佈稅率
      that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end           計算。
      of the reporting period.

   Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                       遞延稅項按綜合財務報表內資產及負債賬面
   between the carrying amount of assets and liabilities in the              值與計算應課稅溢利所用相應稅基之間的暫
   consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax               時差額確認。遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課
   bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax             稅暫時差額確認。遞延稅項資產一般按可能
   liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary            有應課稅溢利用作扣減可動用可扣減暫時差
   differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for             額為限，就所有該等可扣減暫時差額確認。
   all deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is             倘若暫時差額由不影響應課稅溢利或會計溢
   probable that taxable profits will be available against which             利的交易中初次確認（業務合併除外）資產及
   those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such              負債所產生，則不會確認該等遞延稅項資產
   deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the             及負債。此外，倘暫時差額乃因初次確認商
   temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other           譽而產生，亦不會確認遞延稅項負債。
   than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in
   a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the
   accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not
   recognised if the temporary difference arises from the initial
   recognition of goodwill.

   Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary             遞延稅項負債乃就與附屬公司及聯營公司投
   differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and               資有關的應課稅暫時差額確認，惟倘本集團
   associates, except where the Group is able to control the                 能夠控制暫時差額的撥回及暫時差額於可見
   reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that              將來可能不會撥回則除外。
   the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable
   future.

   The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the             遞延稅項資產的賬面值於報告期末進行檢
   end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that                討，並扣減至不再有足夠應課稅溢利可供收
   it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profit will be           回全部或部分資產。
   available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.

   Deferred tax liabilities and assets are measured at the tax               遞延稅項負債及資產按預期於結算負債或變
   rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the               現資產期間適用的稅率計量，所根據的稅
   liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate (and     率（及稅法）乃於報告期末已頒佈或已實際頒
   tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively enacted by              佈。
   the end of the reporting period.

   acquisition or issue of financial assets and financial liabilities        損益（
                                                                               「按公平值計入損益」 ）的金融資產或金
   (other than financial assets or financial liabilities at fair value       融負債除外）直接應佔的交易成本於初步確
   through profit or loss (“FVTPL”)) are added to or deducted                認時於金融資產或金融負債（如適用）的公平
   from the fair value of the financial assets or financial liabilities,     值中加入或減去。收購按公平值計入損益的
   as appropriate, on initial recognition. Transaction costs                 金融資產或金融負債直接應佔的交易成本即
   directly attributable to the acquisition of financial assets or           時於損益中確認。
   financial liabilities at FVTPL are recognised immediately in
   profit or loss.

   The effective interest method is a method of calculating                  實際利率法乃計算金融資產或金融負債的攤
   the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability            銷成本以及於有關期間分配利息收入及利息
   and of allocating interest income and interest expense over               開支的方法。實際利率乃於初步確認時按金
   the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate              融資產或金融負債的預計可使用年期或（如
   that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                 適用）較短期間內準確貼現估計未來現金收
   payments (including all fees and points paid or received that             入及款項（包括構成實際利率不可或缺部分
   form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction         的所有已付或已收費用及積分、交易成本及
   costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected               其他溢價或貼現）至賬面淨值的利率。
   life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where
   appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on
   initial recognition.

      (ii)   Definition of default                                           (ii)   違約定義

   (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                  (iii) 信貸減值金融資產
         A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more                   金融資產在一項或以上違約事件（對該
         events of default that have a detrimental impact on                     金融資產估計未來現金流量構成不利
         the estimated future cash flows of that financial asset                 影響）發生時維持信貸減值。金融資產
         have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is credit-               維持信貸減值的證據包括有關以下事
         impaired includes observable data about the following                   件的可觀察數據：
         events:

          (a)    significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the            (a)   發行人或借款人的重大財務困
                 borrower;                                                              難；

          (b)    a breach of contract, such as a default or past due              (b)   違反合約（如違約或逾期事件）；
                 event;

          (c)    the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                   (c)   借款人的貸款人因有關借款人財
                 contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                         務困難的經濟或合約理由而向借
                 financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower                   款人批出貸款人不會另行考慮的
                 a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not                           優惠；
                 otherwise consider;

          (d)    it is becoming probable that the borrower will                   (d)   借款人將可能陷入破產或其他財
                 enter bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation;                    務重組；或
                 or

          (e)    the disappearance of an active market for that                   (e)   由於財務困難導致於金融資產活
                 financial asset because of financial difficulties.                     躍市場消失。

   (iv)   Write-off policy                                                 (iv)   撇銷政策
          The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                    當資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財困及無
          information indicating that the counterparty is in severe               實際收回可能時，例如對手方被清
          financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of              盤 或 已 進 入 破 產 程 序 時（以 較 早 者 為
          recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been                   準），本集團則撇銷金融資產。經考慮
          placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy                 法律意見後（倘合適），遭撇銷的金融
          proceedings, whichever occurs sooner. Financial assets                  資產可能仍須按本集團收回程序進行
          written off may still be subject to enforcement activities              強制執行活動。撇銷構成取消確認事
          under the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into                      項。任何其後收回在損益中確認。
          account legal advice where appropriate. A write-off
          constitutes a derecognition event. Any subsequent
          recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.

      (v)  Measurement and recognition of ECL                              (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
           The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability             預期信貸虧損的計量取決於違約概
           of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the           率 、 違 約 虧 損 率（即 違 約 時 的 虧 損 程
           loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.            度）及違約風險承擔。違約概率及違約
           The assessment of the probability of default and loss               虧損率的評估乃基於過往數據及前瞻
           given default is based on historical data and forward-              性資料作出。預期信貸虧損的預估反
           looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an                  映無偏概率加權金額，以各自發生違
           unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is                    約的風險為權重釐定。
           determined with the respective risks of default occurring
           as the weights.

            Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                   一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據合約
            contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                應付本集團的所有合約現金流量與本
            accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the           集團預期收取的現金流量（按於初始確
            Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective              認 時 釐 定 的 實 際 利 率 貼 現）之 間 的 差
            interest rate determined at initial recognition.                   額。

            Interest income is calculated based on the gross                   利息收入根據金融資產的賬面總值計
            carrying amount of the financial asset unless the                  算，惟金融資產為信貸減值的情況除
            financial asset is credit-impaired, in which case interest         外，在此情況下，利息收入按金融資
            income is calculated based on amortised cost of the                產的攤銷成本計算。
            financial assets.

            The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in                 本集團通過調整所有金融工具的賬面
            profit or loss for all financial instruments by adjusting          值於損益內確認其減值收益或虧損，
            their carrying amount, with the exception of trade and             惟相應調整於虧損撥備賬中確認的應
            other receivables where the corresponding adjustment               收貿易賬款及其他應收款項除外。
            is recognised through a loss allowance account.

      Derecognition of financial assets                                    取消確認金融資產
      The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the               本集團僅於資產收取現金流量的合約權利已
      contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire,          到期，或金融資產已轉讓及本集團已將其於
      or when it transfers the financial asset and substantially all       金融資產所有權的絕大部分風險及回報轉移

      entity. If the Group retains substantially all the risks and         保留已轉讓金融資產所有權的絕大部分風險

21. BIOLOGICAL ASSETS                                                21. 生物資產
   A – Nature of activities                                            A – 業務性質
   The biological assets of the Group are dairy cows held to           本集團的生物資產為持有作生產原料奶的乳
   produce raw milk.                                                   牛。

   The quantity of dairy cows owned by the Group at end of the         以下是本集團於報告期末擁有的乳牛數量。
   reporting period is shown below. The Group’s milkable cows          本集團的奶牛是養來產奶的乳牛。小母牛及
   are dairy cows held for milk production. Heifers and calves         小牛都是未滿產奶年齡的乳牛。
   are those dairy cows that have not reached the age that can
   produce milk.

                                                                                    2020年             2019年

    Heifers and calves                            小母牛及小牛                           111,168            98,433


    Total dairy cows                              乳牛總計                             247,371           233,618


   The Group is exposed to fair value risks arising from changes       本集團要承受原料奶價格變動帶來的公平值
   in price of raw milk. The Group does not anticipate that            風險。據本集團預計，在可預見的將來原料
   the price of the raw milk will decline significantly in the         奶價格不會大幅下跌。董事認為，本集團並
   foreseeable future and the Directors are of the view that there     無可用的衍生工具或其他可以訂立的合約用
   is no available derivative or other contracts which the Group       於管理原料奶價格下跌的風險。
   can enter into to manage the risk of a decline in the price of
   the raw milk.

   In general, the heifers are inseminated with semen when             一般情況下，小母牛於長至約十四個月大時
   heifers reached approximately 14 months old. After an               進行配種。約九個月的孕期過後，小牛出
   approximately nine months’ pregnancy term, a calf is born           生，奶牛開始生產原料奶及哺乳期開始。奶
   and the dairy cow begins to produce raw milk and the                牛通常會擠奶約340天，之後進入約60天的
   lactation period begins. A milkable cow is typically milked         乾乳期。
   for approximately 340 days before approximately 60 days dry
   period.

      When a heifer begins to produce raw milk, it is transferred         當小母牛開始產原料奶時，乃按轉撥日期的
      to the category of milkable cows based on the estimated fair        估計公平值轉撥至奶牛類別。
      value on the date of transfer.

      The Group is exposed to a number of risks related to its            本集團面臨與其生物資產有關的多種風險。
      biological assets. The Group is exposed to the following            本集團面臨以下運營風險：
      operating risks:

      i.       Regulatory and environmental risks                         i.    法規及環境風險
               The Group is subject to laws and regulations in the              本集團須遵守其開展農場種植及育種
               location in which it operates plantation and breeding.           活動所在地的法律及法規。本集團已

               environmental and other laws. Management performs                查以識別環境風險及確保現有系統足
               regular reviews to identify environmental risks and              以管理該等風險。
               to ensure that the systems in place are adequate to
               manage these risks.

      the consolidated statement of financial position as deferred     期內按直線法計入損益。
      income and credited to the profit or loss on a straight-line
      basis over the useful lives of the related assets.

37. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                            37. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in              本集團的資金管理旨在確保本集團旗下實體
   the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                 的持續經營能力，同時透過優化債務及股本
   while maximising the return to stakeholders through the               結餘為利益相關者謀求最大回報。本集團的
   optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s              整體策略較去年保持不變。
   overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.

   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which        本集團的資本架構由債務淨額（包括銀行借
   includes bank borrowings and other borrowings as disclosed            款及其他借款（分別披露於附註29及30），
   in Notes 29 and 30 respectively, net of bank balances and             扣除銀行結餘及現金）及本公司擁有人應佔
   cash and equity attributable to owners of the Company,                權益（包括已發行股本、股份溢價及儲備）組
   comprising issued share capital, share premium and reserves.          成。

   The Group’s management reviews the capital structure                  本集團的管理層定期檢討資本架構。作為檢
   on a regular basis. As part of the review, the management             討的一環，管理層會考慮資本成本及各類資
   considers the cost of capital and the risks associated with           本涉及的風險。本集團透過發行新股份及發
   each class of capital. The Group will balance its overall capital     行新債務或贖回現有債務等方式保持整體資
   structure through the issues of new shares and the issue of           本架構的平衡。
   new debt or the redemption of existing debt.

   (ii) Interest rate risk                                               (ii) 利率風險
        The Group is exposed to cash flow interest rate risk due              本集團因銀行結餘、已質押銀行存款
        to fluctuations in the prevailing market interest rates               及按浮動利率計息銀行借款的現行市
        on bank balances, pledged bank deposits and bank                      場利率波動而承受現金流量利率風
        borrowings which carry interest at variable interest rates.           險。

         The Group’s fair value interest rate risk relates primarily        本集團的公平值利率風險主要涉及定
         to fixed-rate bank and other borrowings and lease                  息銀行及其他借款及租賃負債。
         liabilities.

         The Group manages its interest rate risk by performing             本集團通過定期審查利率風險淨額，
         regular reviews of the Group’s net interest rate                   並可能訂立利率掉期合約（如需要），
         exposures and may enter into interest rate swap                    以管理其利率風險。於2020年12月31

   Directors are satisfied that the Group will be able to meet in              綜合財務報表已按持續經營基準編製。
   full its financial obligations as and when they fall due in the
   foreseeable future. Accordingly, the consolidated financial
   statements have been prepared on a going concern basis.

 40. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS                                               40. 公平值計量
      Fair value of the Group’s biological assets, financial                 按經常性基準以公平值或公平值減銷售
      assets and financial liabilities that are measured at fair             成本計量的本集團生物資產、金融資產
      value or fair value less cost to sell on a recurring basis             及金融負債的公平值
      The Group’s biological assets are measured at fair value               本集團的生物資產按公平值減出售成本計
      less costs to sell, other financial assets, equity investment at       量，其他金融資產、按公平值計入其他全面
      FVTOCI and derivative financial instruments are measured at            收入的權益投資及衍生金融工具於各報告期
      fair value at the end of each reporting period. The Directors          末按公平值計量。董事已成立估值團隊，由
      have set up a valuation team, which is headed up by the                本公司財務總監領導，以釐定適當的估值技
      chief financial officer of the Company, to determine the               術及公平值計量的輸入數據。
      appropriate valuation techniques and inputs for fair value
      measurements.

      In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group     估計資產或負債的公平值時，本集團使用可
      uses market observable data to the extent it is available.             用市場可觀察數據。倘第一級及第二級的輸
      Where Level 1 and Level 2 inputs are not available, the Group          入數據不可用，本集團會委聘第三方合資格
      engages third party qualified valuers to perform the valuation.        估值師進行估值。估值團隊與合資格外聘估
      The valuation team works closely with the qualified external           值師緊密合作，為模型確立合適的估值方法
      valuers to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and          及輸入數據。首席財務官每半年向董事呈報
      inputs to the model. The chief financial officer reports the           估值團隊的發現以闡釋資產及負債公平值波
      valuation team’s findings to the Directors semi-annually to            動的原因。
      explain the cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets
      and liabilities.
